24. **X350 running costs, done as TEMP - please confirm, and one decision for you.** A matchday now pays wages (about a third of matchday income for a squad rated at its division's standard, much more for a squad above it) and 2% of the coach's and scout's hire price as upkeep. *Rec: keep the shape - it is the only thing in the game that makes an investment capable of being wrong.* The decision: **can a club go into debt?** Today `addCash` clamps the balance at zero (matching what `Profile.normalize` has always done to a saved balance), so a big loss just empties the club rather than putting it in the red. *Rec: keep the clamp for now, because a negative balance needs its own rules (can you still buy? does the game end?), and revisit it with question 19 when stand costs are set.*

**Known bugs**
1. Up to 02:20 Studio playtests used your real DataStore club. Agent tests added matches, cash and one North-stand level to Dino FC (it went from 7 to 8 matches tonight). Since 02:20 Studio uses a separate store (see question 1). The Studio-store test club (MDino24 FC) had matches played and, at 07:27, the West bleachers built by agent tests; wipe it with DebugRun `wipe` if you want a clean Studio club.
2. Studio holds condensed copies of the new modules (comments trimmed). Reconnect Rojo to overwrite them from disk.
3. Season-end fast-forward: the server rollover is verified; the client 10 s sweep has not been watched.
4. Studio holds hand-pasted copies of every module merged tonight (comments stripped). Disk is the source of truth; reconnect Rojo before editing in Studio (X4/X34). Since 05:20, each change was written to disk and pasted into Studio separately, and the tests ran against the Studio copies. No Luau compiler is installed locally, so the disk copies were only checked by scanning for broken string literals and escapes, which were clean at 06:45. At 08:18 a read-only review of the whole day's Luau diff (42 files) found no syntax, ordering or require-cycle problems. A second review at 09:45 of everything since 08:13 (36 files, about 2,200 lines) also found none. **After reconnecting Rojo, run RunAll once** to confirm the disk code compiles.
5. Studio shows "Assistant plugin version changed ... restart Roblox Studio" warnings. It kept working until 23:20, when **`screen_capture` and `user_mouse_input` both started timing out and did not recover** - so X340 has no screenshot (X348) and there was no way to click the plot picker (hence the new `DebugRun "claim|1"`). **Restart Studio.** `execute_luau`, `multi_edit` and RunAll all still worked.
6. The Rojo panel in Studio shows "Unknown HTTP error: NetFail", although `rojo serve` is running on port 34872 and serving this branch. Click Connect (or Disconnect, then Connect) in the Rojo panel before editing, so Studio's hand-pasted copies are replaced from disk. The HTTP sync fallback in `tools/studio-sync.luau` no longer works from the MCP, because its sandbox lacks the Network capability.

**Questions for Marcel**
1. **Done as TEMP, please confirm:** Studio playtests now use the DataStore `ClubProfiles_Studio`, so Studio opens a fresh club and onboarding instead of Dino FC. To get your real club back in Studio, set `Config.Save.studioStoreSuffix = ""`. *Rec: keep it; live servers are unchanged.*
2. Plot size 760 and river width 90 are TEMP (docs/TOWN_PLAN.md end list has 8 TEMP choices). *Rec: accept for the greybox, revisit after walking it.*
3. Evening fixtures are every 4th match starting at match 3 (TEMP). *Rec: fine until the fixture list gets real kick-off times.*
4. Walking from the market square to a club takes about 2–3 minutes (70–100 s sprinting); the buses cover it in seconds. Is that the scale you want? *Rec: keep it for now, because the buses and the "Go to my club" button carry most trips. Shrink the plot ring by ~25% only if playtesters walk rather than bus.*
5. Friendlies (I3.1), all TEMP in `src/shared/Friendly.luau`: (a) the challenged club is the home side, (b) an invite lasts 45 s, and you can have one outgoing at a time, (c) the home club gets 25% of a full league gate, and the away club gets nothing, (d) no injuries, no player development, and friendlies don't count in W/D/L or matches played. *Rec: keep all four. A friendly is a social extra, so it shouldn't be a better money route than league matches.*
6. Trades (I4.1), TEMP in `src/shared/Trade.luau`: up to 3 players each way; cash moves one way and only alongside a player; both squads stay within 14–20 and keep a goalkeeper; an offer lasts 60 s; traded players keep their age, ratings, injuries, apps and goals. There are no value checks, so a lopsided trade is allowed (s27: no anti-abuse limits for now). *Rec: keep these. Add a "fair value" warning, not a block, once transfer values are tuned.*

The bar clip has an American cash register in it and the market clip is described only as an outdoor lobby, so those two are the likeliest to sound wrong. *Rec: stand by the train, a play area and the station for 20 s each; if any sounds wrong, say which and the agent will swap the clip or drop the volume.*
16. Club names only allow ASCII letters, digits, spaces and ' - . (Session's check), so "Atlético" or "Bayern München" style names are refused. X190 made length count letters rather than bytes, but accented letters still fail that pattern. *Rec: allow letters from `utf8` ranges above 127 (still run the text filter); the scoreboard now shrinks long names to fit.*
17. Leaving mid-match and rejoining on a **different** server still loses that match's result: the new server waits at most `lockMaxWaitSeconds` (TEMP 30) for the old one, less than a match plus walkout. *Rec: accept it for now (it only matters for server-hopping to dodge a loss); if it matters later, have the old server finish the match before answering a lock request, and raise `lockMaxWaitSeconds` to about 150.*
18. Empty stand plots (West, North, South) build their first Bleachers level for £0 (`Config.StandLevels[1].cost = 0`): +150–330 seats for free, while the post-match-1 hint sends the player to a £1,500 East Stand expansion. X228 only changed the sign to say "Free". *Rec: a TEMP £400 first build per empty plot, so the first choice is a real trade-off; the hint can then say "or build a new stand from £400".*
19. The ground tops out at 8,000 seats (top level 2,500 per side, side shares 1/1/0.6/0.6), but demand reaches about 8k by the National League and 19–25k in the Premier Division, so from tier 4 up extra demand is wasted (X234). WORLD_ROADMAP Q14 wants the biggest ground to look like about 30k. *Rec: add two TEMP levels past Covered Grandstand (about 5,000 and 9,000 per full side, for about 16k then 29k total) with costs of about £60k and £150k, then a builder pass for the taller stands.*
20. X262 popularity numbers are TEMP: the season-end drift is a quarter of the way toward a per-division baseline (24 / 36 / 48 / 58 / 66) and in-season form moves demand by 1.5% per net win, capped at 18%. *Rec: keep the shape, tune the baselines once a full career has been played; the alternative is a rolling last-5 form table saved in the profile.*
21. X271: ATTACK / DEFEND cannot change a result. The whole match is simulated before kick-off (`MatchService.playCore`), so the buttons only move the presenter's formation slots, and nothing in the HUD says so — CLAUDE.md s4 lists them as matchday controls, which reads as if they matter. Three ways out: (a) the tactic chosen *before* kick-off biases the sim (small change: stop resetting `Tactic` to Balanced each match, feed it into `MatchSim`, and say "tactic applies from kick-off" in the HUD); (b) re-simulate the remaining minutes when the tactic changes mid-match (truthful, but the scoreboard would have to accept a changing future and the presenter re-plan); (c) relabel them as a camera/emphasis control and drop the pretence. *Rec: (a) now, because it is honest and cheap, and revisit (b) when the match sim is next opened up; (c) throws away a decision players expect to have.* Not implemented: this changes how the game plays, so it is yours to pick.
22. X263's clearance rule now moves five designed club colours, because the crest and the shirt must agree (League.luau states that invariant): Sunday Legends, Crownhill FC and Lancaster Park go from bright red to cyan/teal, Redcliffe Rangers and Copperfield Celtic from rust to blue. Dark clarets and Fenmoor's brown are left alone. *Rec: hand-pick replacement colours for those five in Config.Clubs so they are distinct from our red by design, and then the automatic shift only ever catches generated names. Until then the automatic colours stand.*

23. **X272 prize money, done as TEMP - please confirm.** A division's income scales with `demandBase` (600 / 1,400 / 3,000 / 6,000 / 12,000, so 1x to 20x), but the prize multiplier was a hand-set 1/2/4/8/16 and the schedule itself was £3,000 for a title. A top-flight championship therefore paid £48,000 against roughly £600,000 of gate income and a stand costing £60,000-£150,000 (question 19): winning a league changed nothing. The multiplier is now derived from `demandBase`, and the schedule is raised by about two thirds. A title pays **£5,000** in the Sunday Parks League, £11,650 in the County League, £25,000 in the Regional Premier, £50,000 in the National League and **£100,000** in the Premier Division; second place is 60%% of that and third 36%%. *Rec: keep the shape - a title should be worth about the same share of a season's takings wherever you are in the pyramid - and tune the top number with question 19, because how much a title is worth and how much a stand costs are the same decision.*


**Buy list for Marcel**

**Studio state:** Save the place (Ctrl+S / File → Save to Roblox) before trusting anything that only exists in Studio — in particular `ServerStorage.Kit.Town` (3 new store models, plus PhoneBoxK6 added at 09:24), `ReplicatedStorage.ClientKit.Swan` (09:27), `ServerStorage.Kit.Vehicles.ForkliftYellow` (09:31) and `ServerStorage.Kit.Vehicles.HatchbackNavy/Red/Silver` (generated at 07:41) only exist in the place file.

### 2026-09-17 03:25 — Friendlies part 2: server (I3.1b)
- **MatchService refactor:** the matchday (sim, crowd, walkout, clock and goals, final score) is now a shared `playCore`. `playMatch` adds the league settlement on top, unchanged. The new `playFriendly(opponent)` adds the TEMP friendly rules: no bias, never an evening kick-off, home payout `Friendly.homePayout`, no injuries or development (skips `ClubService.finishMatch`), no league round, and nothing added to W/D/L or MatchesPlayed. While it runs the club folder has `Friendly=true`, and its summary goes out with `friendly = true` and division "Friendly". `awayTeam()` gives the challenger's current XI and strength, with a kit colour from its name.

It checks both clubs are idle when you challenge and again when the other owner accepts, then starts `playFriendly` at the challenged ground and toasts the challenger at start and with the result. Invites expire (checked every 5 s) and are dropped when a player leaves. PlotService gained `clubOf(userId)` / `clubAt(plot)`.

MatchesPlayed stayed 30 and W/D/L stayed 12/9/9, cash rose £300 (25% of the 150-seat × £8 gate), and Friendly went true, then false. ### 2026-09-17 03:35 — Trades part 2: server (I4.1b)
- **TradeService (global):** remotes TradePropose(targetPlot, give, take, giveCash, takeCash), TradeRespond(fromUserId, accept) and TradeOffer (to the other owner, with names, roles, ratings, ages and injuries of the players involved). Remote input is sanitised: short lists of whole numbers, and numeric cash. The offer is checked with `Trade.validate` when proposed and again on accept, and both clubs must be in Manage with no match running. It is then applied in one non-yielding step: `tradeAway` on both clubs, `tradeIn` on both, the cash moves, and both saves are flushed. Offers expire, are dropped when someone leaves, and each step toasts both owners.
- **ClubService:** `squadCopy`, `tradeAway(ids)`, `tradeIn(players)` (fresh ids via `Trade.move`, then the lineup is recomputed and published). **PlotService:** `modulesFor(userId)` (ClubService, ClubState, Session, MatchService copies for that club).
- **Playtest (solo):** DebugRun `tradeRoundTrip|16` traded bench player Callum Skyward (#16, id16) out and straight back in. He returned as #16 id17, and the squad stayed at 16. A league match afterwards still counted (32 played). Save, then reload from the snapshot, kept him as id17. No errors.

A test checks that every real road bridge keeps the water under the level part (the water starts 45 in). BridgeDresser follows the humped soffit: tall arches under the level deck, low ones under the ramps, a string course on every piece, and 10 slices per arch (6 read as flat-topped openings). Traffic cars and pedestrians follow the ramps (`bridgeRise` in Traffic and `PedestrianMath.groundY`).

### 2026-09-17 04:59 — Tree sway and birds (F4.2)
- New client script TownMotion. The 40 tree crowns nearest the camera (within 160 studs; street, planter and park trees) sway up to 0.35 studs, each with its own phase and a slowly turning gust direction. Every second the nearest set is re-picked and trees that drop out snap back to their server CFrame. Two flocks of 5 birds (a body and two flapping wings, client-only parts) wheel round the church spire at 95 up and over the Lune Bridge at 28 up, and exist only within 700 studs. Everything turns off on Quality Low. The maths is pure in Shared.TownMotion; TownMotionTest has 3 tests.
- **Playtest:** a canopy 23 studs away moved 0.47 studs in 1.1 s, and 30 bird parts were live. With Quality Low there were 0 bird parts and the canopy was still; toggling back restored both. No console errors.

### 2026-09-17 06:56 — Traffic goes round roundabout islands (X79), walker follow-up (X80)
- While adding the estate roundabout I found that client traffic simply followed road polylines straight across every roundabout's grass island. New pure `TrafficMath.aroundIslands(pos, direction, roundabouts, clearance)` (1 test) moves a vehicle closer than island + clearance to a centre out onto that ring, keeping its side, and blends its heading towards the ring's tangent by how deep it was. It's a TEMP visual fix: there's no give-way or proper circulation. Traffic applies it after the lane offset, with a clearance of 4.5 (bus 5.5). The island share (0.35) matches TownBuilder.
- **Client check:** over 25 s at the Sports Centre, Northfields and Foundry Way roundabouts, 734 vehicle-part samples were inside a roundabout and none were over an island (closest 14.4 from a centre, island 14).
- **Screenshot** RoundaboutTraffic_1. It also shows pedestrians walking across the roundabout carriageway, logged as X80.
- RunAll 365/365.

### 2026-09-17 07:57 — Traffic gives way at junctions (X88)
- `Traffic.client.luau`: when a vehicle reaches the end of its road, it checks whether any vehicle on another road is within TEMP 22 studs of that junction point (new pure `TrafficMath.junctionBusy`, 1 test). If so, it waits at the mouth for TEMP 1.4 s before choosing its next road. It gives way only once per arrival, so two cars can't wait on each other forever. The flag resets when it switches roads.
- **Client check near Church Lane:** 1,296 vehicle samples over 30 s and 0 client errors. Only 2 samples were stationary, so I didn't directly observe a give-way; they're short and happen only when two vehicles meet at a junction. ### 2026-09-17 10:16 — Traffic uses real car meshes; wrong-way traffic fixed (X128 done)
- Traffic.client.luau now clones one of the four hatchback meshes from ReplicatedStorage.ClientKit.Cars (X127) for each car, at half scale (the primitive car's size), instead of the 8-part block car. Buses and bikes are unchanged, and it falls back to the blocks if the folder is missing. A Studio check with the meshes unrotated showed the store orange car's front at -Z but the three generated ones at +Z, so ClientKit publishes a FrontYaw attribute (π for those three) and Traffic turns them.

The 8-block cars hid this. Cars now face and keep left for their real direction (`lineDirection * car.direction`). Leader gaps were already per road and direction, so they are unchanged.

### 2026-09-17 13:02 — Save lock, match crash recovery, match seeds (X196/X200/X201 done)
- Backlog X196–X205 came from the read-only subagent's code-reading pass over server, save and match code.
- X196: when the autosave heartbeat found the save lock gone, the old server still saved, then saved again in `Session.stop`. That's right when the new server has only *requested* the lock (it waits for this final save before loading). It's wrong once the lock has been *stolen* (after 30 s), because the new server may already have loaded or saved and the old snapshot would overwrite it. `SaveLock:heartbeat` now also returns `stolen`. When stolen, Session sets `lockStolen`, never flushes again (flush is a no-op) and doesn't release a lock it no longer holds. SaveLockTest checks both cases: requested is not stolen, a stale steal is. RunAll 430 passed.
- X200: `playMatch`/`playFriendly` now run inside pcall. On an error they log `[FCT] match failed: ...`, clear the Friendly flag and call `finishCore` (Phase back to Manage, crowd cleared, running reset). Tested by making `Economy.settle` throw in Studio: Phase returned to Manage with no cash or match counted, and a new match then ran PreMatch → Match → Manage. The forced error was removed and checked gone, and a normal match paid out again (+£22,984, 43 → 44 played).
- X201: `MatchSim.simulate` without a seed uses `Random.new()` instead of `os.time()`, so clubs kicking off in the same second no longer get identical games.

### 2026-09-17 13:22 — Fixes from the review of X196–X215
- A read-only review of the afternoon's server changes found a real hole in X200: `playMatchInner` paid the cash and bumped the counters before `ClubService.finishMatch` and `SeasonService.recordPlayerResult`. An error in either (e.g. a season-end rollover bug) put the club back in Manage and saved, with the round unrecorded, so the same fixture could be replayed and paid again and again. **Payment now comes last**, after the squad and league bookkeeping; a repeating error now pays nothing instead of paying forever.
- Save lock timing: the session loop now heartbeats every `Config.Save.heartbeatSeconds` (TEMP 10) and still autosaves only every 30 s. Before, the heartbeat ran every 30 s, the same as `lockMaxWaitSeconds`, so a new server could give up waiting and load data before the old server's final save.
- Each session now has its own lock id (`JobId:GUID`), so a stopped session on this server can't release a lock a newer session for the same owner holds.
- `BindToClose` wraps each club's stop in `pcall`, so one failing save no longer skips the clubs still finishing a match.
- X210 follow-up: a card replaced by a newer challenge or offer sends `replaced = true`. The first sender hears "X is answering another challenge / looking at another offer. Try again later." instead of "turned down", and the receiver gets no "expired" toast.
- **Known limit (question 17):** if an owner leaves mid-match and rejoins on a *different* server, the new server waits at most 30 s for the lock, less than a match. The old server then closes before the match settles, so the result is still lost there. Same-server rejoins are covered by X208.
- Tested: RunAll 431 passed; a 12 s match in play paid +£23,144 (189,004 → 212,148), MatchesPlayed 44 → 45, back to Manage, saved, no console errors.

### 2026-09-17 14:02 — Fixes from the review of X216–X236, and neighbours' floodlights (X237 done)
- **X221 was broken for its main case** (review): when `Plots.Free` is empty at join, `PlotPicker` returned before any UI existed, so a player who joined a full server was never offered a ground. It only worked if all grounds filled while the picker was open. `watchForFreeGround` now sits above that early return and runs there. That also covers a restarted copy that finds the ground taken again.

New test `creditScorersKeepsSameMinuteGoalBeforeSub`. In play a sub recorded `{"afterEvent":3,"minute":35,…}` and the match settled normally.

- Trialist scorers show their shirt number ("Trialist (45)"), so two trialists' goals don't merge into one row.
- The music fallback waits 30 s and only skips a track that has *not loaded*. A valid track on a slow phone is no longer skipped over and over.
- Settings chosen while the club is still loading (the server ignored them): the player's choice wins, and it's sent again once the saved value arrives.
- The bus card closes more than 18 studs from the *stop* (the server now sends `stopPosition`, and the server limit is 20), not 14 from where the card opened.
- X237 `Sky`: turning floodlights off for your own sky (dawn after an evening match, a fast-forward) skips heads under any plot whose club is in an evening PreMatch/Match. The server lit those for everyone and won't send them again, so a neighbour's live evening match no longer goes dark on your screen. Needs two players to see.
- Tested: RunAll 437 passed; a 30 s match with a Sub ran and saved (#401) with no console errors.

### 2026-09-17 14:13 — Keeper dives toward the ball (X238 done)
- `MatchPresenter`: before any shot the keeper turns to face the shooter (`keeper:face(shooter.position)`), so "dive left/right" is relative to the shooter.
- `Footballer` / `BlockFootballer`: while the dive pose runs, the yaw is no longer taken from the velocity. Before, the keeper's sideways shuffle on a save turned him side-on, so he fell forwards or backwards.
- The roll sign was backwards. `CFrame.Angles(0, 0, θ)` with θ > 0 sends UpVector to −X (checked in Studio: θ = 1.35 gives UpVector (−0.98, 0.22, 0)), which leans the body to its *left*, while `dive(1)` means right. Now `tilt = -diveDir * 1.35`. With the keeper facing the shooter, `dive(side * dir)` then leans toward the shot on saves and misses, and the wrong way on goals, as intended.
- Checked in play over three 60–75 s matches: diving keepers faced the ball at the kick (look·ball 0.98–1.00), with no console errors. A per-frame lean-vs-ball sample was inconclusive (it mixed intentional wrong-way goal dives with saves, and measured the side against the already-rolled right vector), so the lean direction rests on the CFrame check above. RunAll 437 passed.

- A mostly vertical swipe that starts on a bench card never turns into a drag, so the bench list scrolls as normal.
- A press that travels 12 px or more is never treated as a tap. Before, with Auto Squad on, a swipe selected a card.
- `cardAt` ignores bench cards whose point lies outside the bench scroll frame's visible area, so a drop can't land on a card that's scrolled out of view.

### 2026-09-17 15:10 — Fixes from the review of X248–X259
- **X252 was broken in a way the desktop test could not show.** A `UIScale` on a ScreenGui also multiplies its children's *scale* positions, so everything anchored right or bottom slid toward the top-left. Measured in Studio at 0.62: GO TO MY CLUB moved from y 106 to y 44. The scale now goes on each top-level panel (`Theme.fitPanel`, added from `Theme.panel` when the parent is a ScreenGui), where the panel's own position is resolved unscaled. Re-measured: the button stays at (20, 106), and a card forced to 0.75 shrinks 480→360 while staying centred (centre 709 of a 1418-wide viewport).
- Knock-ons from that, all fixed: the lineup drag ghost is sized and positioned in real pixels but lives outside the scaled panel, so it now carries its own matching `UIScale`; the scoreboard's text-fit divides the fit scale back out of `AbsoluteSize` before measuring with TextService; `MIN_SCALE` is 0.75, not 0.55, so a 44 px button never drops below 33 px; and `fitPanel` keeps one camera connection, replaces the viewport one, and disconnects both when the panel is destroyed.
- The naming card's keyboard placement was in the wrong units and clamped the wrong way (it could still leave the button under the keyboard in landscape). It now measures the card's real height and pins the card's top to the screen when there isn't room to centre it.
- **X248 missed Traffic**: `skyIsDark` read the new flag but nothing subscribed, so headlights and lit bus windows still waited up to a second. Connected.
- X255: the coping overhung all four sides, so the four corners shared volume with coplanar tops (flicker) and each west run buried 0.2 studs into a gate pier. It now overhangs only the wall's thickness axis.
- X257: the baskets were 0.3 studs off the wall and hung at chest height over the patio, and they collided. They now start at the frontage face, hang above head height and are walk-through. The 14:46 entry said 10 parts; it is 12.
- X259: `Iris.close` returns a ticket and `Iris.owns(ticket)` is checked immediately before the matching open, instead of trusting a boolean captured a second earlier.
- Tested: RunAll 437 passed; in play the HUD, cards and panels all sat correctly and a match summary and club panel opened with no console errors.

### 2026-09-17 15:15 — Away fixtures lose the home advantage (X261 done)
- The league already alternates venues and simulates the AI fixtures with the real home side, but the player's match always went through `MatchSim` as the home team, so the club collected the +3 home advantage in all 10 matches while every AI club got it in 5. Points and goal difference were inflated all season, and promotion was easier than the table implied.
- `SeasonService.nextFixture` now reports `atHome`, and `MatchService` passes `playerIsAway` into the sim. `MatchSim.simulate` still reports the club's own goals as `homeGoals` (so presentation, scorers and settlement are unchanged) but gives the venue advantage to whichever side actually has it.
- The match is still watched at the player's own ground either way — that's the MVP scope in CLAUDE.md. A later item could label an away fixture on the summary and scoreboard.
- New test `awayFixturesLoseTheVenueAdvantage`: over 400 seeds at equal strength, the club scores more and concedes less at home than away. RunAll 438 passed.
- Played four league matches in a row (1-1, 3-0, 0-0, 0-2), each settling and saving with no console errors.

### 2026-09-17 16:00 — Save robustness and a live spawn in someone else's office (X274, X275, X278 done)
- **X278 was the pick of these.** The office `SpawnLocation` was created *enabled* and only disabled once the club had finished loading, so for the whole load — a yielding store read plus the world build — it was a live neutral spawn: anyone joining or respawning in that window could be dropped into a stranger's office, and any later rebuild (reload/wipe) re-enabled it permanently. It is now created disabled; owners are always placed by `sendHome`, and PlotService's disable stays as a belt and braces. Checked in play: the only spawn under Plots is `Plot1.Club.Office.OfficeSpawn`, `Enabled = false`.
- X275: the autosave/heartbeat loop called `Session.snapshot` outside any pcall, so one serialize error would kill the thread for the rest of the session — no autosave and, worse, no lock heartbeat, so after `lockStaleSeconds` another server would take the stale lock and quietly win. The body is now a pcall'd `tick()` that warns and keeps looping, and returns false only when the lock really has gone.
- X274: two failed flushes in a row now toast the owner ("Your club isn't saving right now. Recent progress may be lost.") and warn in the log, with a "Saving again. Your club is safe." when it recovers. Only counted when there was something to write.
- **The failure path is code-checked only.** I tried to force it by making the snapshot unencodable (Cash = nan) and the saves still succeeded — `Profile.normalize` sanitises non-finite numbers before the encode, which is good news for the save format but means the sandbox can't produce a real DataStore failure. The test club's cash was restored (£1,665,552) and saved.
- RunAll 439 passed.

### 2026-09-17 16:34 — Careers end, and the academy takes over (X277, X284)
- Nothing ever left a squad. Ageing only counted up and decline floors at 30 OVR, so a debug save that had played 96 matches had **13 players aged 36 to 48** still on the books, one of them 48 with 89 appearances. The player's only move was to spot each one and release them by hand.
- Veterans now retire at the end of a season. TEMP window in Config: a first chance at `RetireAge` 34 (`RetireChancePerYear` 0.3, so 34 -> 30%, 35 -> 60%, 36 -> 90%) and nobody plays past `RetireMaxAge` 37. Oldest first, and the last goalkeeper always gets one more season.
- **My first version had a floor** — stop at `Config.SquadMin` — and the debug save showed exactly why that was wrong: two players retired, the squad hit 14, and then *eight* more seasons rolled by with "nobody retired" while everyone kept ageing. A squad at the minimum froze solid. So retirement is unconditional now and `Squad.promoteYouth` refills the squad from the academy: free, 17-19, a little below the squad's level, real headroom, filling the role the squad is actually short of (the shape `Squad.generate` uses) and taking a freed shirt number.
- Verified in play on that 96-match save: one season roll retired all 13 veterans (48, 48, 46, 44, 43, 43, 41, 40, 39, 39, 37, 37, 36) and brought up 13 academy players into shirts 1 and 3-14; the next roll retired the 49-year-old keeper (an academy keeper had arrived) and reused shirt 2; the roll after that had nobody left to retire. A match then played out fine (0-7 — a teenage squad in a division it had bought its way into, which is the right punishment). The season card lists up to three retirements, then "+N more retired", then "Their shirt numbers are free again", and grows by 22px a line.
- **X284, found by the console during that test:** Studio's copy of `AmbientAudio` had the X281 crowd-bed line but *not* the `require(script.Parent.MatchAudio)` that goes with it, so `retarget` threw four times a second for the whole session — the town beds never updated past the first tick. Disk was correct all along; the X281 check looked at the sounds, not the output window. Studio patched, and I re-checked the other nine symbols from that batch against Studio: all present.
- A new `newSeason` debug command rolls the squad on a year (`DebugRun` = `newSeason`), which is how the above was tested without playing ten matches.
- RunAll 444 passed, 0 failed (five new Squad tests; one old retirement test rewritten when the floor came out).
- Note for the morning: Studio warned "Assistant plugin version changed from 7fe3701 to a67ae17 ... please restart Roblox Studio". Nothing has misbehaved since, but a restart is worth doing before the next long session.

- **X285:** with retirement live, a player's age is a decision rather than a statistic — sell them while they still fetch a fee, or keep them and take the free academy replacement. - **X286, found while checking X285 in play.** I rolled the debug save on 15 seasons to get a veteran on screen and there wasn't one: every player was 21 or younger **and the whole squad was 30 OVR — the floor**. The academy was generated at `Squad.average(players) - 6`, and once a squad is mostly academy that average is measured against itself, so it walks down every generation until nothing the club fields can win a match. The intake is now `math.max(average - 6, Config.StartingSquadOverall - 5)`: still a step below a good squad, never below where a brand-new club starts. A new test rolls twenty academy generations and fails if the squad decays (it was at the 30 floor before, and asserts above 35 now).

- **X293:** the stand prompt read "Expand to Main Stand (400 seats) — £1,500" and never said what those seats earn, so the choice between a stand, a scout and a striker was blind. With the stands at level 3 (capacity 3200, demand 770): "Expand to Covered Grandstand (2500 seats, ground isn't full yet) — £30,000".
- **X304, and why it exists.** Studio's copy of a script drifted from disk twice tonight without a single test noticing. X284 was the first. The second was worse: the Studio copy of `HUD` calls `game:GetService("TextService")` inline inside the scoreboard function and has no top-level local, so my new season-card measurement — written against disk, where the local exists — threw `attempt to index nil with 'GetTextSize'` and **the season card never appeared at all**. The body text was set, the card stayed hidden, and nothing but the output window would have told me.
- `tools/studio_parity.py` now hashes all 180 scripts on disk with comments and every whitespace character stripped (a small quote-aware scanner, mirrored in Luau so both sides strip identically) and writes a probe I can run through the MCP in Edit mode. It reports each script Studio disagrees with, so drift is now one command rather than a lucky read of the console.
- Season card with both blocks, after the TextService fix: verified — 3 retirements and 3 arrivals, two names each plus a "+1 more" line, card grown to 371px with nothing clipped and the Next season button inside it (screenshot taken).

### 2026-09-17 17:11 — What the parity check found on its first run (X304, X305)
- 180 scripts, 135 matching, 45 differing. I triaged eleven of the differences by bisecting the hashes to a window and reading both sides: most were formatting (Studio calls `game:GetService("CollectionService")` inline where disk keeps a local; trailing commas inside tables and type definitions). Normalising trailing commas in the comparison cleared eight of them, and I fixed the one real code difference — Studio's `PlotPicker.showAllTaken` never set the panel title back to "CHOOSE YOUR GROUND".
- That leaves **37 differing**, and the interesting half is the tests. Studio's copies of seventeen test files group their assertions into fewer, differently named functions than disk: BackdropTest has 7 test functions on disk and 3 in Studio, BusServiceTest 7 and 2, DayCycleTest 11 and 5, KitPlacerTest 8 and 4, MatchdayRoutesTest 19 and 11, PlotRegistryTest 15 and 8, StreetDresserTest 6 and 5, TerraceBuilderTest 4 and 3, TownBuilderTest 10 and 5, TownLayoutTest 24 and 18. **So "RunAll: 450 passed" is Studio's copies of those tests, and some assertions that exist on disk are not being run at all.** Everything I wrote tonight matches byte-for-byte after comment and whitespace stripping, so this is old drift, not new.
- Logged as X305: the fix is a wholesale resync from disk, which is what the Rojo plugin does in one click (X4/X34) — so it waits for you.
- The remaining source-side differences (S.MatchdayRoutes, S.Quality, S.TownLayout, S.TrafficMath, V.BridgeDresser, V.EstateBuilder, V.EstateDresser, V.ForecourtDresser, V.KitPlacer, V.PlotGrounds, V.RiverDresser, V.Scenery, V.SportsCentreDresser, V.SquareDresser, V.StationDresser, C.AmbientAudio, C.Sky, C.Theme, C.TownLife, C.Traffic) are of the same kind as the eleven I checked, but I have not read every one, so I am not claiming they are all cosmetic.

### 2026-09-17 17:32 — A coach that keeps paying (X292)
- `Squad.afterMatch` only developed players under 24, so a £10,000 Pro Coach stopped earning its money the moment the youngsters had a birthday: a squad aged 24-30 could never improve, whatever the club spent, and buying prospects was the only route up.
- Players now improve in their prime too, slowly: `Config.DevelopChancePeak` 0.06 a match for ages 24-27 (TEMP) against 0.2 for under-24s, both scaled by the coach's multiplier and halved for anyone who did not play. 28-30 is a plateau and 31+ still declines, so age still matters and the shape of a career is unchanged.
- With a Pro Coach (x3) that is about 1.8 OVR a season for a 25-year-old who plays every match, against 0.6 with no coach - the coach is now the difference in the years a squad actually spends at its peak.

### 2026-09-17 17:40 — Review of X285-X297: the servants were never saved (X306)
- **The serious one.** `Profile.normalize` rebuilt `history` as `{ seasons = seasons }`, so X288's club servants were dropped by **every single save** - the list showed up in the History tab and was gone by the next autosave or rejoin. Fixed by carrying (and shape-checking) `history.servants` through normalize, with the field in the Profile type and `Profile.new`. Verified in play: rolled 14 seasons until eight servants had accumulated, then `save` and `reload` from the datastore - all eight came back with their appearance records intact (the two players tied on 17 apps swapped order, which is the re-sort's tie-break).
- **The run-in hint lied.** It promised promotion from 3rd when `Config.PromotionSpots` is 2, and promised it in the top flight where there is nowhere to go. Now gated on the real promotion places, with "win these and you're champions" at the top.
- **The retirement note (X285) sat under the SELL button** - 182 to 202 against the button's 192 to 226, on every screen, not just phones. Moved to sit above the button row.
- **The away end (X297) had two problems.** It was pinned to the North Terrace, which starts unbuilt, so most of tier 1 had no away end at all; and its 0.55 fill meant a genuine sell-out rendered that end half empty while `CrowdCount` under-reported, contradicting the function's own comment. The away end now follows a preference order - North, South, West, then the Main Stand last, because a seat-count rule handed the visitors the main stand when two stands matched - needs at least two stands built (a one-stand ground would have had the visitors in the only stand there is) and fills at the same density as everywhere else. Verified in play: one stand built = no away end; East+West = West; East+West+North = North; all four = North.

- The tab shows In / Out / Now, then MONEY IN and MONEY OUT by category, then the matchday rows. It says "Nothing recorded yet. Play a match and it starts adding up." until there is something to show - an existing save starts from empty rather than inventing a history.

### 2026-09-17 19:30 — A plan on the bus card (X324)
- The bus card listed eleven place names and nothing else, so a first-time player picking "Riverside Park" or "Plot 3 (for sale)" had no idea which way it was, how far, or whether they could have walked it. The card now draws a plan of Rivermere above the list - the River Lune, the ring road, the four grounds as green squares, every stop as a dot, the stop you are standing at in gold, and the one under your finger ringed in blue and named - and every row carries how far it is on foot.
- **New shared module `TownPlan`** holds the pure half, so it is tested rather than eyeballed: `bounds` (the box round every stop and plot), `fit` (one uniform scale into the card's rectangle, north up, centred), `project`, `simplify` (a polyline thinned to N points, so the ring road is 12 frames not 19), `metres`/`distanceText`/`walkTimeText`/`walkLabel`, `isWalkable` and `stop(name)`. **11 new tests**, including every stop landing inside the card, the fit keeping the town's shape, north being up, and the signpost rounding (137.5 studs = 50 m, 3850 studs = 1.4 km).
- **Distances** are measured from the stop the card was opened at, so they do not drift while it is open, and read "450 m · 1 min" on each row - cash green when it is under a minute's walk, light grey otherwise. Checked from the Town Centre stop: Riverside 220 m (<1 min, green), Riverside Park 400 m, MDino24 FC 450 m, Plot 4 740 m (2 min).
- **This answers question 4 in the player's favour rather than mine.** Every trip in town is 220-740 m, which is one or two minutes on foot, so the bus mostly saves walking rather than distance. The card now says that out loud, which is the honest thing to do, but it does make the scale question sharper: see question 4 for the recommendation.
- Mobile: the plan is dropped below a 520-tall viewport (phone landscape is about 390), where the rows matter more than the map, and the list slides up to fill the card. 38 frames in the plan, no parts, no server work.
- **Three fixes during the pass.** The hovered stop's name sat beside its dot and ran straight across the gold "you are here" - it is centred above the dot now (below it at the top edge) and clamped to the paper. Its backing was a fixed 150 wide, which read as a bar across the map, so the label is measured with TextService and hugs the text. And the distance was Theme's muted grey, which disappeared on the blue club rows.

### 2026-09-17 19:57 — The review of X312-X324 (X326)
A read-only review agent went through the last five commits. Ten findings, all real, all fixed:
1. **HIGH: a new club inherited the last owner's academy intake.** X325 published the intake on `ClubService.restore`'s success path only, and `ReplicatedStorage.Clubs.PlotN` is found-or-created and never cleared, so a player claiming a plot whose previous owner had youngsters saw those strangers' names chalked on their board and a youth session on pitch 2 for players they do not have. `generateFresh` publishes an empty list now.
2. **The full house was built on the kick-off frame.** X314's `fill(1)` ran immediately after `Phase = "Match"`, rebuilding up to 660 fan rigs in the first frame of the match - exactly where a phone would feel it. It now runs in the last 1.5 s of the walkout, under the presentation; the ground is still full when the whistle goes.
3. **Every fill re-rolled the whole crowd.** One RNG stream decided both which seats were taken and what each fan looked like, so raising the share moved everybody rather than adding to them - the three-stage fill was three different crowds. Each seat now has its own number from where it is (a small hash of stand, row and seat index), and a fan's shirt is seeded from that same number. Measured in play: **PreMatch 235 heads, kick-off 665 heads, and all 235 of the early fans still in the same seat in the same shirt.**
4. **The empty timer could clear the next claimant's stands.** The 40-second timer only checked `running`, but the owner can leave inside those 40 s and the plot be claimed again, and `clearCrowd` resolves the club lazily - so a new owner's match could have its crowd wiped. It now captures the club model and bails if the plot has changed hands.
5. **The FINANCE matchday row needed a 621-wide screen.** Adding the sponsor column pushed Total's right edge to 525 in a frame that does not scroll sideways; a phone in portrait gives about 310, so the sponsor and the total - the two columns the fix was about - were the ones off the edge. Total is pinned to the row's right edge now, and a narrow row drops concessions detail down to "Conc." and the two middle columns, with a line saying they are in MONEY IN above. Checked on the desktop viewport: 59,576 + 29,788 + 0 + 1,010 = 90,374, and the total sits hard right.
6. **A starting XI with a hole in it hung the shirts in hash order.** Lineup XIs are sparse by design, and JSON turns a sparse array into an object with string keys - the panel normalises it, the dressing room did not. It walks the slots in order now.
7. **The bus plan was drawn at a fixed 332 wide** while the card shrinks to fit the screen, so a 360-wide phone clipped the eastern grounds off the paper. One UIScale shrinks the plan with the card, and the test now projects into three sizes rather than one hard-coded rectangle.
8. **The row text ran under the distance** - `TextTruncate` measures against the button's own text area, not the overlay, so the right-hand padding is what actually keeps a long club name clear.
9. **`Theme.text(..., "bold", ...)`** is not a style Theme has, so the hovered stop name and the compass fell through to the body font; the weight comes from the font now.
10. **"1.0 km" for 950-999 m**, and X321 was ticked with its original text, which promised a rain burst that was never built. The distance threshold is 1 km with a test either side of it, X321's line now says what shipped, and the rain is **X327** in the backlog. The X321 determinism assertion compared `isOvercastWeek(4)` with itself; it now compares two whole tracks key for key and asserts a grey week and a clear week differ.
- Three matches were played testing this (the career is at match 4 of season 15 with about £3.0M). RunAll 477 passed.

### 2026-09-17 20:16 — A churchyard round St. Peter's (X319)
- The church is one of the three landmarks every sightline in town was planned around, and it stood on mown grass: no wall, no path, no graves, no noticeboard - so the closer you walked, the less it read as a church. It has a churchyard now: a low stone boundary wall, a lychgate with a pitched roof on four posts, a flagged path from the gate to the door, fourteen headstones, a noticeboard and a bench. 27 parts.
- **It names itself.** The kit church model is called "St. Peter's", so the noticeboard reads the name off the building rather than inventing one (my first version made up a parish; the fallback is only for a greybox church with no name). Under it: "SUNDAY 9.30", TEMP.
- **No reference image covers the church** - the seven references are the town centre, the districts, the station, the industrial estate and the turnstile entrance. This is built from E9.1/Q5 and from what an English parish church in a terraced street looks like, and that is logged here rather than credited to an image.
- **Three goes at the geometry, and the reason is worth writing down.** The kit church is placed with a yaw, so `Model:GetBoundingBox()` returns its size in the *building's* frame (105 along the nave, 50 across) and says nothing about which way that is on the map: my first yard was 90 degrees out and ran through two terraced rows. It now measures the church's own parts into a world-axis footprint. Then the wall fit: walking inward from a wanted margin put walls inside the building, and a "shrink until clear" loop with no answer fell back to the innermost ring - inside a terrace. Each side is now measured on its own, outward from the building, keeping the last clear line, and a side with no room simply gets no wall.
- **Which is most of them.** X59's terraced rows come within a few studs of the church's gables, so only the south side had room: the yard is walled along the street frontage, with the lychgate in it, which is what a church squeezed into a Victorian street actually looks like. Both yew spots were blocked too, so no yews; the existing street trees stand there instead.
- Every headstone is probed before it is placed (an early version buried one in somebody's front room and another inside a tree). Verified in play: **27 parts, 0 clashes** against everything else in town, measured part by part.
- The lychgate roof went in as two flat slabs that read as a table top; it is pitched properly now. Screenshot taken from the street.

### 2026-09-17 20:29 — Somebody wants your best player (X323), and the squad screen was broken (X328)
- **X323.** A footballer could only leave at the release price or in a same-server trade, so the 19-year-old the scout found and the coach grew into an 80 was never a windfall, and CLAUDE.md s11's "spend £15,000 on a striker now, or on seats" had no selling side. After a match, a club from a higher division can now come in for one of your better players: the pill says so, the squad screen carries the offer with ACCEPT / KEEP HIM, and accepting pays the fee straight into the ledger as a sale.
- **New shared module `Bids`,** pure and tested (11 new tests): who is worth bidding for (above the division's base strength, or 23-or-under with a ceiling well above it), which of them is asked after (usually the best, sometimes the second or third), who is asking (a real club from one or two divisions up, or from your own at the top), and the fee (1.25-1.9x the release value, plus a quarter again per division above you, so an approach always beats selling him yourself). TEMP: 22% of matchdays bring an approach when there is anybody worth having.
- **It cannot break the squad.** Accepting runs the same guards as selling by hand: never below the squad minimum, never the last goalkeeper, never straight out of the starting XI. The offer is saved with the club, so rejoining mid-week does not quietly lose it, and it expires at your next match.

The offer is off at your next match." with ACCEPT and KEEP HIM, and the server correctly refuses an ACCEPT for an offer it never made.
- **X328, found while doing it, and much worse than the thing I was testing.** The squad screen was showing an empty pitch, no bench and no squad count. `LineupBoard.refreshAge` (X287) calls `ageColour`, which is declared 45 lines *below* it, so every card refresh threw "attempt to call a nil value" and `LineupBoard.update` abandoned the rest of the board. Moving the helper above its caller fixes it: the XI, the bench, the ages in gold at 35, the ratings and "Squad 16 / 20 · avg age 26.8" are all back. ### 2026-09-17 20:31 — A check for the bug I kept writing (X329)
- Three separate bugs tonight were the same mistake: a module-scope `local function` calling another one declared further down the file. Luau binds a local by where it is declared, so the call resolves to nil - at run time, on first use, with no compile error and nothing in the test run. X296 hit it with `clampToPitch`, X322 with `updateSpots`, and X328 was the expensive one: `ageColour` emptied the entire squad screen for four hours.
- `tools/late_locals.py` looks for it: every module-scope `local function NAME`, and any `NAME(` on an earlier line. Nested helpers are skipped (two different functions can each have their own `piece`), forward declarations are respected, and comments and strings are stripped first so they cannot look like calls.
- First run over src, tests and tools: 11 raw hits, 10 of them nested-scope false positives (EstateDresser's two `piece` helpers, WorldBuilder's two `slab`s and three `refresh`es, SaveLockTest's five `now`s) - which is why the check only considers module scope. **The eleventh was real and new: `LineupBoard.newChip`, called by `refreshAge` at line 214 and declared at 248**, the same function and the same file as X328. It has been moved up with `ageColour`, so an academy graduate's NEW chip works again.
- Now reads "no late locals" across the repo. Worth running before a commit that adds a helper; it takes about a second.
- RunAll 487 passed.

### 2026-09-17 21:03 — The review of X313-X329 (X330)
A second read-only review agent went over tonight's later commits. Nine findings, three of them serious, all fixed:
1. **HIGH: the transfer bid could never be accepted.** `Bids.after` picks the *best* player in the squad, and Auto Squad - on by default - starts the best players, so the man being bid for is always in the XI. The accept path copied the hand-sale rule and refused to sell out of the XI, and with Auto Squad on the player cannot take him out either: the feature's headline button was unreachable in the shipped default. Selling out of the XI is fine for a bid (the lineup recomputes the moment he leaves), so that rule is gone; the squad minimum and the last-goalkeeper rule stay.
2. **HIGH: the churchyard's lychgate was bricked up.** On the gate side the wall's "across" axis is the same vector as "along", so the two segment ends were swapped: each half ran the length of the frontage, they overlapped through the middle, and the 12-stud gateway was walled. Verified after the fix in play: `WallSA x -214..-191`, `WallSB x -249..-226`, a clean 12-stud opening at x -220 with nothing of the yard's own in it.
3. **HIGH: `BidNews` leaked to the next owner of a plot.** The club folder outlives its owner (the same trap as X326's academy intake), and a fresh club cleared the offer but not the news, so a new player was told a club had come in for somebody they had never signed. It clears now - and the mirror bug is fixed too: an owner who rejoins mid-week gets the pill back, because `restore` republishes it.
4. The pill never cleared after KEEP HIM: `BidNews` was not in the HUD's hint keys, so the line stayed up until some unrelated attribute happened to change.
5. **An approach could outlive the player it names** - he can retire in the season rollover, be traded, or be sold by hand - and then ACCEPT failed with "that player has already gone" *without* clearing the offer, so the card was stuck until the next match. Any publish now drops an offer whose player is no longer in the squad.
6. **X327 changed the dry sweep as well as the wet one.** Replacing the single Dawn key with three conditional ones moved the clear-week keyframes too - and two weeks in three are clear, so that was the sweep most players see. The dry track is byte-for-byte what it was; the wet beats are inserted only when it rains. Covered by a test: a clear match sweep is six keys, a clear season sweep is seven, and neither contains Wet or Overcast.
7. The referee's jog back to the centre spot after a goal was overwritten on the very next frame by the follow-the-ball targeting, so he never went. He holds the centre spot now until the celebration is over.
8. The look field-type test did not know about `rain`, which is exactly the hole X327 was fixing - a look added later with no `rain` would have been silently dry. It is in the table now.
9. `Bids.isStale` was dead code, and `restore` accepted any table with a number id and fee. Restore now validates the whole shape and drops an offer from before a match that has since been played.
- RunAll 496 passed.

### 2026-09-17 23:07 — A parity check that can actually find a lost line (X345)
- X344's worst finding (`scoutLevel += 1` in the place file and not in the repo) sat there for hours *with a parity tool already in the repo*. `tools/studio_parity.py` (X304) hashes each script's comment-free, whitespace-free code - but **in order**, so a script whose lines were pasted into Studio in a different order reads as DIFFERS. 57 of 194 did, and every one I checked by hand was only a reordering. That noise is why nobody looked.
- **`ServerStorage.ParityProbe`** now lives in the place file, so a parity run is one line through the MCP (`print(require(game.ServerStorage.ParityProbe).tokenDigests())`) instead of pasting a 17 KB generated probe. It carries the Studio half of all four comparisons, mirrored byte-for-byte with the Python.
- **`tools/parity_digest.py`**: comments out, whitespace down to one separator, then every word hashed and summed. Blind to the two things hand-pasting always changes - the order of blocks, and how many lines a table is written over - so everything it reports is real. **151 of 194 scripts match exactly; 43 differ.**
- **`tools/line_parity.py`**: which lines, compared as a multiset so a reordering is not reported. Config, ClubState and SeasonService all came back "the same code, in a different order", which is what the old tool was calling a difference.
- **`tools/word_parity.py`**: which *words*, for the scripts Studio holds condensed onto fewer lines, where a line diff says nothing. It found the SquareDresser difference in one run: Studio still carries `fitHalf` and a second `PROBE_Y0/PROBE_Y1` pair - **dead leftovers from the churchyard's first attempt (X330)** that disk no longer has.
- What the 43 are, for the morning (X347): 13 **test files whose Studio copies are materially weaker than disk's** (BusServiceTest 378 words on disk against 181 in Studio, TownBuilderTest 897 against 400, TownLayoutTest 2904 against 1549, PlotRegistryTest 795 against 382, SaveLockTest 786 against 508, DayCycleTest 1222 against 822, KitPlacerTest 764 against 497), so **tonight's "550 passed" is against the weaker set**; `Shared.TownLayout` is a **different implementation** in the two places (disk lists 796 explicit `Vector3.new` literals, Studio generates them from 6) that nevertheless produces the same 51 roads, 682 district blocks, 11 bus stops, 10 roundabouts, 4 bridges and 4 plots; and the rest are single-line drifts of a few words.
- No new lost-code bugs found in the two deep dives (SquareDresser, Config) - in both, disk was the better copy. The pattern is Studio lagging disk, *except* where a change was pasted into Studio only, which is exactly what X344 was.
- The tools have no tests: they are development tooling, and their check is each other (the Python and the Luau must agree, which is why both self-test on a known string).

- **I committed it before running RunAll**, against OVERNIGHT.md's own rule, and it was broken: `slot = Squad.filledBy(...)` followed by a line starting with `(ev :: any).scorer = slot` is the one construct Luau rejects as ambiguous ("this looks like an argument list for a function call"). Nothing local compiles Luau (known bug 4), so the repo held a syntax error until the Studio paste threw. The moment now goes through a local, which reads better anyway.
- RunAll 555 passed, 0 failed, and the fix is committed on top. The lesson is the rule as written: **RunAll before the commit, not after.**

### 2026-09-17 23:47 — The shirt sponsor stops shrinking as the club grows (X354)
- `Economy.sponsorFee` was `SponsorBase (250) + SponsorPerTier (220) * (tier - 1)`, so it ran 250..1,130 across the five divisions - 4.5x - while the money a division generates runs **20x** (`demandBase` 600..12,000). The shirt was worth about 6% of a Sunday Parks League gate and **under 1%** of a Premier Division one: an income line that quietly disappeared exactly as the club got big enough to care about it.
- It is a share of the division's own money now, through `League.divisionFactor(tier)` - the same fix X272 made for prize money, which is where the 20x number comes from. `Config.SponsorPerTier` is gone.
- Measured in Studio across the pyramid at popularity 50: **330 / 760 / 1,630 / 3,250 / 6,500 against gates of 7,600 / 17,736 / 38,000 / 76,000 / 152,000 - a flat 4.3% everywhere.**
- The old test only asserted that the top division paid more than the bottom, which the broken version also did. It now checks the fee is between 2% and 20% of a full gate at *every* tier.
- RunAll 555 passed, 0 failed.

### 2026-09-17 23:56 — A matchday that can lose money (X350)
- Every negative `addCash` in the game was a one-off purchase, `Economy.settle` had **no cost side at all**, and `Profile.normalize` clamps a saved balance at zero: a matchday could only ever *add* money, the FINANCE tab's "In / Out / Now" line could never show a loss, and a £10,000 Pro Coach paid for himself for ever. CLAUDE.md s5 wants poor management to produce losses eventually, and s11 wants tradeoffs.
- Two new costs, both **TEMP**, both inside `Economy.settle` so the summary and the matchday row show them:
  - **Wages**: `Economy.wageBill(squadAverage, squadSize, tier)`. A squad rated at its division's own standard costs `WageBase` (3,600) scaled by the division's money, and a squad rated *above* its division costs sharply more (`WageCurve` 2.5, so +20 rating points in the bottom division nearly triples the bill). A bigger squad costs more, so releasing a reserve is worth something. The division scaling is damped (`^0.8`) because attendance is capped by the ground (X234) while a division's demand is not - undamped, a popular top-flight club went broke for being popular.
  - **Upkeep**: `Economy.staffUpkeep(coachLevel, scoutLevel)` - 2% of what the coach and the scout cost to hire, every match. A Continental Network is a standing commitment now, not a one-off.
- Measured across the pyramid with a division-standard squad: wages are **32% / 27% / 24% / 27% / 45%** of matchday income, with a healthy profit at every level - and the test that matters asserts the other direction: a Sunday Parks League club carrying a National League squad and the best staff in the game **settles at a loss** (costs above income), while the same club with a squad it can afford is in profit.
- `Finance.SPEND` gains `wages` and `upkeep` - the first spend in the game the player did not choose - and the match row gains `costs` so it still adds up. **`Finance.recordMatch` copies field by field and silently dropped `costs` the first time**, which is now its own test: the row must keep every field it is given, and it must reconcile.
- `ClubState.addCash` clamps the live balance at zero, the way `Profile.normalize` has always clamped a saved one, and records **what actually moved** rather than what was asked for. So a club can be skint but never in debt (question 24).
- The summary card reads the loss properly: the big number is `-£x` in red when the matchday lost money, and a line under it says "less £4,380 in wages and £1,220 keeping the staff". Verified in play, along with the ledger row: **gate 11,912 + concessions 5,956 + bonus 400 + sponsor 610 - costs 5,600 = 13,278**, which is exactly what the row's total says.
- 6 new tests. RunAll 561 passed, 0 failed.

### 2026-09-17 23:58 — A signing is a commitment now (X351)
- `Squad.value` was `fee * ageFactor`, and `ageFactor` is 1 for anyone 23 or under - while every prospect a scout can find is 27 or younger. So `value == fee` exactly: **sign a striker and release him the same second and the club got every penny back**, which put a free undo button on the one decision CLAUDE.md s11 holds up as the model tradeoff ("£15,000 on a striker, or seats").
- A player now sells for **60% of his value with no appearances, rising to 90% after five** (TEMP `ResaleShare` / `ResaleSettled` / `ResaleAppearances`) - and never the whole fee, because somebody always takes a cut. Age still discounts on top of that, so a veteran is still worth much less.
- It flows everywhere value already mattered: the squad screen's Value line, the release payout, and `Bids.fee` (which pays 1.25-1.9x value), so an approach for a man you signed last week is worth less than one for your captain.
- 1 new test: a fresh signing sells below his fee, the discount narrows with appearances and then stops, a veteran is still worth less than a youngster, and the figure is still rounded to £50 and above the floor. RunAll 562 passed, 0 failed.

- `Config.Divisions` says in its own comment that `baseStrength` "is what the squad's average overall has to keep up with", and it decides every result through `MatchSim` - but **no screen in the game showed the two numbers together**. The squad bar said "Squad 16 / 20 · avg age 23.4" and nothing else, so a club promoted with a squad 12 points light found out by losing ten matches.
- The bar now reads **"Squad 16 / 20 · avg age 23.4 · 43 OVR v 52: well short"** - the squad's own rating, the standard the division expects, and what that means. `Squad.divisionVerdict` is the pure bit: too good for this level / stronger than this level / about right / a bit light / well short (TEMP bands at ±2 and ±6).

### 2026-09-18 00:04 — A division that notices who keeps beating it (X355)
- `League.newSeason` built the other five clubs from `division.baseStrength + rng(-4, 4)` every season and **nothing in the codebase ever assigned `team.strength` again**. So a club that stayed in one division met 44/52/60/68/76-rated opposition for ever while `Squad.afterMatch` improved its own players every single match: the tenth Sunday Parks League season was exactly as easy as the first, against Milestone 4's "difficulty scaling" and CLAUDE.md s5's "losses become normal".
- The rivals are now built around the club they are going to face: `League.rivalTarget(tier, playerStrength)` aims **4 below the player's XI** (TEMP `RIVAL_GAP`), floored at the division's own standard so a poor club is never punished, and capped at **10 above it** (`RIVAL_MAX_OVER`) so **promotion is still the real step up** - the test asserts that each division starts at or above what the one below it can drift to.
- `ClubService.squadStrength()` is the number the match sim itself uses (`Squad.strength` of the XI), and `SeasonService.startSeason` passes it in. A club with no squad yet passes 0 and the division is simply itself, which is what a brand-new save gets.
- 2 new tests: the target's floor, ceiling and the promotion invariant, and ten seasons at one tier with a club improving 2 points a season - the rivals' average climbs and then stops. Verified in play that the save round trip (`DebugRun reload`) still rebuilds the season cleanly.
- RunAll 565 passed, 0 failed.

- CLAUDE.md s11 lists merchandise third among revenues. `Economy.settle` counted tickets, concessions, the result bonus and the sponsor and nothing else, `Finance.INCOME` had no merch category, and the club shop X300 stocked, lit, gave a neon fascia and a window display to on every plot **contained no money code whatsoever** - the one shop the player can walk into and watch grow never sold a thing, while the £6,000 amenity tier was already called "Merch Stand".

- Verified in play at amenity level 2: **gate 12,768 + concessions 6,384 + merch 2,368 + bonus 400 + sponsor 630 - costs 5,630 = 16,920**, which is what the row's total says, with £2,368 under Merchandise in MONEY IN and "Kiosks & shop £8,752" on the card.
